# Graph Report - my_auto_guide  (2026-11-11)

## Corpus Check
- 1541 files · ~1,339,427 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 21851 nodes · 31475 edges · 1389 communities (1204 shown, 185 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1242 edges (avg confidence: 0.56)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5be361ad`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
